READING CLUB 2000
LINE-BY-LINE GUIDE TO THE INCLUDES FOLDER

This guide covers the files in both screenshots. Each nonblank source line
has an entry. A line can contain several operations or form part of a
multiline SQL statement, PHP expression, or HTML element.

READING ORDER
1. includes/ contains public helpers and layout, plus older admin helpers.
2. includes/portal/ contains staff sessions, security, and shared pages.
3. includes/portal/partials/ renders selected staff interface sections.
4. includes/portal/services/ contains database queries and business rules.

FOLLOW A WORKFLOW THROUGH THE FILES
Public site: functions.php supplies shared helpers, then header.php, navbar.php
and footer.php render the common page layout.
Admin review: portal/bootstrap.php loads the environment and session.
portal/auth.php checks staff access. A service performs the database work.
A partial then displays the resulting list, detail view, or dialog.
Public uploads and portal content images use different code. upload.php saves
public form uploads locally. portal/services/upload-service.php sends supported
portal content images to Cloudinary.
The book donation files describe code present in the project. Their presence
alone does not establish that a public menu currently links to them.
